217.  
6 Häubl, G., & Trifts, V . (2000). Consumer decision making in online shopping environments: The effects of interactive decision aids. Marketing Science, 
19(1), 4–21.  
Häubl, G., & Murray, K. B. (2003). Preference construction and persistence in digital marketplaces: The role of electronic recommendation agents. 
Journal of Consumer Psychology, 13(1–2), 75–91.  
ただし、両研究は従来型の電子的な推薦・比較機能を扱う。生成AIとの自由な対話への接続は本稿の考察である点に留意。 
7 内閣府消費者委員会事務局. (2026). 人工知能（AI）技術の利用と消費者問題に関する専門調査会の検討状況（案） ［第9回資料］ 
8 Thompson, D. V ., Hamilton, R. W., & Rust, R. T. (2005). Feature fatigue: When product capabilities become too much of a good thing. 
Journal of Marketing Research, 42(4), 431–442. https://doi.org/10.1509/jmkr.2005.42.4.431 
9 Yangらの研究は167人による三つの予測課題を扱い、本文は再犯予測課題の結果を紹介している。生成AIによる購買相談や客観的な商
品理解の研究ではな点に注意。 
矢谷浩司. (2026). 消費者の主体性とAI：Human-AI Interactionの視点から［第9回専門調査会資料］. 内閣府消費者委員会 
Yang, M., Kagawa, R., & Baba, Y . (2026). Do user-aligned explanations steer human decisions? Context-dependent influence and 
ethical implications. In Proceedings of the 2026 ACM Conference on Fairness, Accountability , and Transparency (pp. 2385–2415). 
Association for Computing Machinery .〔H14-R01〕

### Page 4

また先掲の内閣府の調査によれば、生成AI利用者のうち、AIの回答をそのまま受け入れてしまう
ことが多いという回答が34.3％と全体の約１/３、そして、AIの回答に納得できなくても自分の意見
を変えることがあるという回答が20.5％であった。 
この結果は、商品やサービスの購入に限らず、日常生活全般での生成AI利用について尋ねたもので
あり、回答を受け入れたり意見を変えたりした理由、 その結果が本人に役立ったかまでは分からない。 
ただし、AIの回答をそのまま受け入れたり、納得できなくても自分の意見を変えたりする利用者が
一定数いることは伺える結果である。

図
表 
 2 
 生成
 AI
利用者の回答受容・意見変更に関する実感

2｜相談 AIの対話の自然さや人間らしさも高まりつつある  
さらに、昨今の生成AIを用いた相談サービスでは、対話の自然さや人間らしさも高まりつつある。 
関連する研究では、AIの人間らしい反応が利用者の信頼を高め得ることや、AIによる説明や説得
が、内容の正誤とは必ずしも一致しない形で利用者の信頼に影響する可能性も指摘されている。11 
これは、AIによる説明が分かりやすく、もっともらしく感じられていれば、その内容が誤っていて
も、 消費者が誤りに気づかないまま、 誤った理解に基づき選択する可能性があることを示唆している。 
今後、相談AIの対人性が高まるほど、その利用促進と併せて、利用者がAIに寄せる信頼が、AIの
実際の能力や回答の確かさに適切に対応しているかも確認する必要があると言えるだろう。

10 Kim, S. S. Y ., Vaughan, J. W., Liao, Q. V ., Lombrozo, T., & Russakovsky, O. (2025). Fostering appropriate reliance on large language 
models: The role of explanations, sources, and inconsistencies. In Proceedings of the 2025 CHI Conference on Human Factors in 
Computing Systems. Association for Computing Machinery . 
11 Tan, L., Li, Z., Huang, Y., Liu, J., Ni, M., Wang, S., Che, J., & Chen, K. (2026). DHCBT: A digital human psychological counseling 
system with a CBT multi-agent framework. Scientific Reports. Advance online publication. 
人間らしい相談インターフェースのシステムが、テキスト型システムより、利用者に「相手がそこにいる」という感覚を強く与え、継続利
用したいという評価を高めた。

### Page 5

ここまで見てきたように、消費者の期待と、製品・サービスの実際の仕様や使い方に違いがあると、
必要な情報を十分に知らないまま購入し、想定した用途に使えなかったり、予想外の費用や負担が生
じたりする可能性がある。そして、この問題は、消費者が十分な情報を得て商品を選べたかという、
消費者支援上の課題にもつながっていく。 
さらに、企業にとっても、場合によっては満足度やNPS（Net Promoter Score）などの顧客評価が
下がるリスクになり得る。

この点を、企業の店頭やサイト運営者の視点から見ると、AIとの事前相談を経た顧客のニーズや期
待を、サイトでの説明や店頭での接客において、どのように受け止めるかという問いにもつながる。 
下表（表1）は、AIとの相談を経た消費者に生じ得る情報の偏りや見落としについて、関連研究を
踏まえ、確認の着眼点として整理したものである。

表 
 1 
 AI
との事前相談を経た商品選びで、確かめたい偏り・見落とし

このように、消費者がAIとの事前相談を経たとしても、情報の偏りや見落としが残る場合がある。 
しかも、企業はその相談内容を十分に把握できるとは限らない。仮に、「企業が提供する AI」を通
じて生まれた期待であれば、設計や権限によっては、相談内容や履歴を参照し、説明を点検する手掛
かりを得られるだろう。一方、顧客が個人で使うChatGPTなどの汎用的なAIサービスでは、その相談
内容を企業が十分に確認できるとは限らない。 
また、AIとの事前相談内容を「個人的なもの」と感じて、事業者への開示に抵抗感を示す消費者も
いると思われる。AIとの事前相談の内容を詳しく聞くことにこだわらず、本人がストレスを感じるこ
となく話せる範囲の情報をもとに、必要な対応を検討していく視点も必要であろう。

着眼点 
  偏り・見落としの可能性
おすすめの前提
 AIに伝えた用途や予算、使う環境が十分でなく、
おすすめが本人の実情に合っていない。
比較する候補
 AIが薦めた商品だけを検討し、自分に合うほかの選択肢が
比較から抜けている。
比較する基準
 価格や性能を中心に比べ、重さ、収納、手入れなど、
 暮らしに重要な点を見落としている。
情報の正確さ
 説明に納得していても、商品の仕様や注意点に誤りや
 抜けがないかを確かめていない。
日常での使い方
 機能や仕様の違いは分かっていても、使う際の作業や手間、
 継続してかかる費用まで想像できていない。
　出典：Häubl & Trifts（2000）、Häubl & Murray（2003）、Thompson et al.（2005）、Kim et al.（2025）を踏まえ
 　　　　 ニッセイ基礎研究所で整理。

### Page 6

その前提に立ったとき、企業はどのような打ち手が考えられるのだろうか。 
まず、消費者自身に、AIとの事前相談を通して重視した点を振り返り、自分の暮らしに大切な点が
比較に含まれていたかを、自ら確かめる、気づける機会を提供していく方法が考えられる。 
例えば、掃除機を例とすれば、機種を価格や吸引力で選んだと話す消費者に対して、本体の重さ、
収納時に必要なスペース、ゴミ捨てやフィルター清掃などについても比較したかを、必要に応じて具
体的に尋ねるような方法で想定される。 
さらに、期待と実際に違いがありそうな点を、図や動画、具体的な使用場面で説明することも、消
費者自身が違いに気づくきっかけになり得る。掃除機なら、ゴミ捨てやフィルター清掃を実演して見
せることで、 「想像より手間がかかる」と分かる場合があるだろう。 
これらはいずれも、従来の顧客対応の延長上で対処できるアプローチでもあるとも言える。

もう１つは、企業でよく行われている消費者（顧客）に対する満足度やNPS（Net Promoter Score）
といった顧客調査の活用する方法も考えられる。 
店舗やサイトで説明を受ける直前（AIとの事前相談をしていれば、その後）に、その商品をどのよ
うに使えると思っていたか（事前期待） 、購入時の店頭やサイトの説明で分かったこと（理解） 、使用
後に感じた期待との違い（事前期待の実態の差についての気づき）を聞く。そして、事前のAI相談に
ついては、その有無のみを併せて聞く。 
これにより、事前のAI相談が期待にどう影響し、期待をどう形成したかまでは分からないものの、
企業にとって商品の改善が必要なのか、店頭やサイトでの説明や相談を補う必要があるのか、を考え
やすくなる。消費者支援の施策面でも、消費者にとってどのような場面で情報が不足しがちなのか、
既存の説明や相談の過程のどこを見直せるかを知る材料にはなる。

こうしたアプローチは、購入時・使用時への対応を中心としてきた従来の顧客へのアプローチに対
して、自社との接触前に行われる「消費者とAIとの事前相談」までをスコープに組み入れ、相談、購
入時の説明、使用後の経験を一体として捉える対応とも言えるだろう。

本稿では、消費者によるAIへの事前相談を出発点に、店舗やサイトでの説明、使用後の経験を一体
として考え、今後の消費者への支援に活かすアプローチに着目してきた。 
次稿では生命保険の調査と聞き取りを取り上げ、既存の説明や意向確認との関係から、この課題を
より具体化して考えていく。

本資料記載のデータは各種の情報源から入手・加工したものであり、その正確性と完全性を保証するものではありません。 
また、本資料は情報提供が目的であり、記載の意見や予測は、いかなる契約の締結や解約を勧誘するものではありません。

Topics  Loss‑making firms are on the rise in China's growth sectors

In China, the number of companies operating in the red is increasing. And the rise is particularly striking in growth 
sectors. One factor behind the increase is the intense competition among firms, which has led to a situation in which 
only the strongest thrive. Another is that many next‑generation growth companies, despite being viewed as having great 
potential, are losing money.

■ Loss‑making firms are on the rise in growth sectors 
In recent years, the number of companies in China's 
growth sectors that are operating in the red has 
increased markedly. The share of loss-making firms in 
all listed firms (below, "loss‑making‑firm ratio") rose 
sharply from 9.6% in 2019 to 26.9% in 2025. Looking 
at the industry distribution of loss‑making firms, from 
2019 to 2022, the ratio increased mainly in 
non‑manufacturing sectors such as real estate and 
accommodation and eating/drinking services, 
reflecting the property market downturn and strict 
stay‑at‑home measures implemented to curb the spread 
of COVID‑19. Later, from 2022 to 2025, the 
loss‑making-firm ratio surged in 
information/communications equipment and 
electronic parts/devices; general, production, and 
commercial machinery; electrical machine ry; and 
chemicals. These four sectors alone accounted for 
around 60% of the overall increase. 
They are the principal growth sectors in Chinese 
manufacturing. This is evidenced by the fact that the 
growth rates of value added in all four sectors exceed 
the average for Chinese manufacturing. Many of the 
high‑tech manufacturing industries that the Chi nese 
government has prioritized for development are 
included in these sectors. For example, 5G base 
stations and semiconductors fall under 
information/communications equipment and 
electronic parts/devices. And robots fall under general, 
production, and com mercial machinery. Meanwhile, 
solar cells and batteries fall under electrical machinery, 
and biopharmaceuticals and organic chemical 
materials fall under chemicals.  
Looking at recent developments in the 
manufacturing of information/communications 
equipment and electronic parts/devices, the global 
communications standard began shifting from 4G 
(LTE) to 5G services in around 2019, and ChatGPT was released in November 2022. These developments 
have provided tailwinds for the growth of the sector. Recently, as it has become clear that ordinary 
consumers and companies can make use of AI services on a daily basis, demand for AI‑related devices and 
semiconductors, as well as d ata centers, has soared. In this environment, many firms are focusing on 
strengthening development, manufacturing, and sales, and the sector's value added has continued to 
increase, rising from 780 billion yuan in 2022 to 910 billion yuan in 2025. Yet over  the same period, the 
number of loss‑making firms increased from 113 to 198, and the loss‑making‑firm ratio jumped from 17.2% 
to 30.1%. 
■ Competition among companies means only the strongest thrive 
One reason loss‑making firms are increasing despite operating in growth sectors is the intense 
competition among companies. The fight for market share has led to overheated investment competition

2 
and price competition. In information/communications 
equipment and electronic parts/devices manufacturing, 
the rate of growth of non -current assets from 2022 to 
2025 (+35.4%) far exceeded the growth rate of value 
added (+16.9%). This indicates that supply capacity is 
expanding faster than demand. In addition, the 
consumer price index (CPI) increased by just 0.4% 
over the same period. Service prices rose 2.2%, 
reflecting wage increases, while goods prices fell 0.7% 
due to excess supply in manufacturing. 
Information/communications equipment prices 
decreased by 3.5%, larger than the drop for goods 
overall. 
As the selection of the fittest through competition 
among companies has progressed, profits have become 
concentrated at a small number of highly competitive 
firms. The share of profits earned by the top 1% of 
firms in information/communications equipment and electronic parts/devices manufacturing rose sharply 
from 24.0% in 2022 to 43.3% in 2025. Leading companies are simultaneously establishing positions as 
both "domestic champions" and "global champions."  
■ Next‑generation growth companies are viewed as having great potential even though they 
are losing money 
However, loss‑making firms cannot simply be lumped together and labeled as losers. Globally, investors 
have shown increasing interest recently in companies with high growth potential even if they are operating 
in the red, and China has a considerable number of such firms. 
In information/communications equipment and 
electronic parts/devices manufacturing, one out of 
every two loss‑making firms is a company attracting 
strong investor interest. The Shanghai Composite 
Index rose 28.5% from 3,089 points at the beginning of 
2023 to 3,969 points at the end of 2025. 20 of the 198 
loss‑making firms in 2025 were not yet listed in 2023, 
and if these are excluded, there were 178 companies 
operating in the red as of 2025. Of these 178 firms, 96 
(53.9%) saw stock‑price increases over the period 
2023-2025 that exceeded the rise in the Shanghai 
Composite Index.  
One characteristic of these 96 companies with strong 
relative stock‑price performance is their active 
investment in R&D and intangible assets. Their 
average R&D intensity (R&D investment / sales) in 
2022 was 9.6%, and their ratio of intangible assets to 
value added stood at 60.5%. Intangible assets include 
computer software, patents, copyrights, and 
relationships with customers and suppliers. In contrast, 
the 82 loss‑making firms with weak relative 
stock‑price performance had an R&D intensity of only 
6.5% and an intangible‑asset‑to‑value‑added ratio of just 38.9%. 
As the above has shown, the increase in loss‑making firms in China's growth sectors is notable, and the 
factors behind it include not only the selection of the fittest as a result of competition among companies but 
also the emergence of next‑generation growth firms that are viewed as having great potential. 
  (Shinichi Seki)

Topics  Climate change heightens the risk of growth stagnation in Asia

Climate change could elevate the risk of economic growth flatlining in the ASEAN zone and India by causing labor 
productivity growth and capital accumulation to stall. To mitigate or avert such a scenario, establishing a business 
environment and institutions that promote "adaptation investment" by both the public and private sectors is an urgent 
task.

■ Heavy‑rain and flood damage is becoming increasingly serious in Asia 
Huge downpours and floods 
resulting in human casualties have 
been a frequent occurrence in Asia in 
the past several years. Still fresh in the 
memory is the debris flow that 
occurred in August this year near the 
border between Nepal and China, 
which as of t he time of writing has 
been recognized as a major disaster 
with thousands dead or missing. And 
in November of last year, Southeast 
Asia and South Asia saw 
record‑breaking torrential rains, 
resulting in numerous casualties, 
particularly in Indonesia and Sri  
Lanka. 
One factor that heightens the risks 
of such floods and heavy rains is 
climate change. As temperatures rise, 
the amount of water vapor in the 
atmosphere increases. Therefore, 
global warming could intensify 
torrential rainfall. Although such risk 
is shared a cross Asia, the way its 
effects are felt differs by region. 
Within Asia, the ASEAN zone and 
India are heavily affected by 
monsoons (seasonal winds), with 
many areas experiencing concentrated 
rainfall during the wet season. In such 
areas, warming can furthe r intensify 
monsoon‑driven heavy rains, 
increasing the risks of floods and 
landslides.  
■ Climate change will delay the escape from the "middle‑income trap" 
Climate change not only results in human casualties by way of natural disasters, but may also undermine 
the long‑term economic growth potential of ASEAN and India through various channels. The Asian 
Development Bank (ADB) [2024] conducted a quantitative an alysis of the extent to which climate change 
will weigh on Asian economies over the long term. Based on the IPCC's "very high GHG emissions" 
scenario (SSP5‑8.5), the analysis assumes a 4.7°C rise in the Earth's average temperature by 2100 relative 
to pre-industrial levels, and estimates the difference in real GDP between that and a hypothetical scenario 
without climate change. The results illustrate the long‑term impact on economic growth through declines 
in labor productivity, stagnation in capital accumulation, and downw ard pressure on consumption due to 
sea‑level rise, floods, heat waves, and other phenomena caused by climate change.  
An examination of the results reveals that in every country/territory, the pushdown effect on real GDP 
relative to the no‑climate‑change scenario expands over time, with 2035, 2050, and 2070 each seeing 
greater impact. The pushdown effect for ASEAN and Ind ia is −15.0% to −8.8% in 2050 and −30.2% to

−18.1% in 2070, larger than the effects for China (2050: −4.8%, 2070: −10.3%) and Central Asian countries 
(2050: −1.7%, 2070: −3.8%). Within Asia, the ASEAN zone and India can be considered regions where the 
risk of economic stagnation due to climate change is relatively large.  
This may make it even more difficult for ASEAN countries and India to escape the "middle‑income trap." 
Although their income levels have risen, they still remain far from high‑income status. Indonesia, Malaysia, 
the Philippines, and Thailand became middle‑income countries in the 1990s or earlier, but have still not 
been able to join the groups of high‑income countries. Furthermore, Vietnam and India, which became 
middle‑income countries in the 2000s or later, have yet to enter a high‑growth trajectory like China once 
did. Climate change, by causing labor productivity growth to stall amid rising temperatures and companies 
to curtail investment due to more frequent disasters, may lead to income growth in the ASEAN zone and 
India being delayed further.  
■ Improving the environment to accelerate "adaptation investment" will be important 
To alleviate the pushdown effect of climate change on growth, expansion of "adaptation investment" by 
the public and private sectors is essential. Specifically, this could include improving working environments 
and promoting measures to lighten workloads a nd reduce headcounts in order to offset declines in labor 
productivity caused by rising temperatures, as well as developing infrastructure and strengthening facilities 
to prevent damage to production equipment from sea‑level rise and floods. However, ASEAN  and India 
cannot be said to have sufficiently 
developed the foundations for public 
and private promotion of such 
adaptation investment.  
As part of its Global Adaptation 
Initiative (ND‑GAIN), the University of 
Notre Dame (U.S.) evaluates countries' 
ability to use investment to achieve 
climate‑change adaptation in the form of 
a "readiness" score with three 
components: economic readiness, 
governance readiness, and social 
readiness. Respectively, these 
components assess 1) whether a 
business environment conducive to 
adaptation investment by private -sector 
companies has been put in place, 2) 
whether the effectiveness of institutions

and policies to support private-sector adaptation investment has been secured, and 3) whether tangible and

Looking at readiness in 2024, ASEAN countries and India generally rank in the middle to lower tiers 
globally, with a large gap between them and developed countries. Notably, the Philippines, India, and 
Thailand fall outside the top 100, indicating weak momentum for adaptation investment. Breaking down 
the scores, problems are especially evident in the economic and governance categories. India and Thailand 
have particularly low eco nomic readiness, ranking 113th and 124th, respectively, and five countries 
(Malaysia not included) fall outside the top 100 for governance readiness. Given this situation, to accelerate 
private‑sector adaptation investment, India and Thailand in particular  need to move swiftly to improve the 
financial environment to make it easier to procure necessary funds domestically and internationally, relax 
regulations to encourage the launch of new businesses, and reform labor markets to enable the smooth 
securing of human resources. In addition, for Asia as a whole, it will also be important to boost confidence 
in the institutional factors that enhance application of investment for adaptation by increasing policy 
stability, ensuring the effectiveness of legal systems, and curbing corruption.

(Tomohiro Hosoi)

CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng không Taseco (AST) 
Tiếp tục mở rộng điểm kinh doanh 
22/09/2026  
Chuyên viên cao cấp Nguyễn Ngọc Anh 
(+84) 24-7303-5333 anhntn@kbsec.com.vn

KQKD tăng trưởng tích cực trong 
2Q2026

Tăng trưởng khách qua cảng hàng 
không thúc đẩy doanh thu tại các 
điểm kinh doanh hiện hữu

Mở rộng mạng lưới tạo động lực tăng 
trưởng doanh thu mới

Cơ cấu kinh doanh và hiệu quả vận 
hành hỗ trợ cải thiện biên lợi nhuận

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA, giá mục 
tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu

2Q2026, AST ghi nhận doanh thu và LNST đạt 479/92 tỷ VND (+22%/19% yoy), với 
động lực chủ yếu đến từ (1) đóng góp từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn 
Nhất và (2) tăng trưởng tích cực của các mảng hàng miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood 
và phòng chờ thương gia. Lũy kế 6T2026, doanh thu và LNST đạt 1,035/221 tỷ VND 
(+33%/46% yoy), lần lượt hoàn thành 48%/49% kế hoạch năm.

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượng khách quốc tế/nội địa tại các thị trường AST khai thác 
tăng bình quân 11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030. Lưu lượng và tỷ trọng khách quốc tế 
gia tăng được kỳ vọng thúc đẩy số giao dịch và doanh thu bình quân trên mỗi 
khách, đặc biệt tại mảng miễn thuế, bán lẻ và phòng chờ.

Chúng tôi dự phóng số điểm kinh doanh AST tăng từ 145 điểm cuối năm 2026 lên 
182 điểm năm 2030. Sau đóng góp từ 19 điểm tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất và 6 điểm mới tại 
T2 Nội Bài từ 2025 - 2026, các dự án Long Thành, Phú Quốc và Gia Bình tiếp tục mở 
ra dư địa mở rộng mạng lưới trong trung và dài hạn cho doanh nghiệp.

Việc mở rộng các hoạt động mang lại lợi nhuận cao, bao gồm hàng miễn thuế, 
phòng chờ thương gia và tối ưu hoạt động tại các điểm kinh doanh được kỳ vọng 
tác động tích cực lên biên lợi nhuận của AST. Chúng tôi dự phóng biên lợi nhuận 
gộp đạt 64.7%/65.5% trong năm 2026/2027, hỗ trợ LNST công ty mẹ tăng lần lượt 
48%/17% yoy.

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA đối với AST, giá mục tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu, tương 
đương tiềm năng tăng giá 20% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 21/09/2026.

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh

CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng không Taseco là một 
trong những doanh nghiệp có quy mô mạng 
lưới điểm kinh doanh lớn trong lĩnh vực dịch 
vụ phi hàng không tại Việt Nam, với 141 
điểm kinh doanh tại 6 sân bay quốc tế tính 
đến cuối tháng 6/2026. Hoạt động cốt lõi của 
công ty bao gồm bán lẻ bách hóa lưu niệm 
(BHLN), hàng miễn thuế, dịch vụ ăn uống và 
phòng chờ thương gia. Ngoài ra, AST còn 
tham gia lĩnh vực suất ăn hàng không thông 
qua khoản đầu tư tại công ty liên kết VinaCS. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Doanh thu trên các điểm kinh doanh hiện hữu tăng nhờ lượng khách quốc tế/nội địa dự phóng tăng 
11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Mở rộng mạng lưới tạo động lực tăng trưởng mới, với số điểm kinh doanh AST 2026 dự kiến đạt 145 
điểm và tăng lên 182 điểm vào 2030. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Biên lợi nhuận gộp cải thiện lên 64.7%/65.5% trong năm 2026/2027 nhờ mở rộng hàng miễn thuế, 
phòng chờ và tối ưu vận hành.

Biểu đồ 5. Doanh thu/điểm kinh doanh của AST

Nguồn:AST

Tăng trưởng khách qua cảng hàng 
không thúc đẩy doanh thu tại các 
điểm kinh doanh hiện hữu 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng tăng trưởng lưu lượng hành khách, đặc biệt là sự gia tăng tỷ 
trọng khách quốc tế, sẽ thúc đẩy doanh thu và hiệu quả kinh doanh của AST 
trong giai đoạn 2026–2030. Theo Cục Hàng không Việt Nam, lượng khách thông 
qua các cảng hàng không trong 7T2026 đạt hơn 76 triệu lượt (+7% yoy), trong 
đó khách quốc tế đạt gần 30 triệu lượt (+11% yoy), trong khi khách nội địa ghi 
nhận tăng trưởng 4% yoy. Xu hướng này được hỗ trợ bởi nhu cầu du lịch quốc 
tế, chính sách thị thực thuận lợi và việc mở rộng mạng bay, trong khi tăng 
trưởng kinh tế và thu nhập tiếp tục tạo nền tảng cho nhu cầu đi lại trong nước. 
Trên cơ sở đó, chúng tôi dự phóng lượt khách quốc tế và nội địa tại các thị 
trường AST khai thác tăng bình quân lần lượt 11% và 5%/năm đến 2030, qua đó 
nâng dần tỷ trọng khách quốc tế trong tổng lưu lượng, thúc đẩy doanh 
thu/điểm kinh doanh nhờ: (1) lưu lượng hành khách qua cảng hàng không gia 
tăng và (2) tỷ trọng khách quốc tế cao hơn hỗ trợ doanh thu bình quân trên mỗi 
khách, đặc biệt tại mảng miễn thuế, bán lẻ và phòng chờ.

lượt) 
 Biểu đồ 7. Doanh thu/điểm kinh doanh AST (tỷ VND)

Nguồn: AST  Nguồn: AST

Mở rộng mạng lưới tại các nhà ga 
hiện hữu và sân bay mới, gia tăng 
quy mô kinh doanh đến 2030 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng chu kỳ mở rộng hạ tầng hàng không giai đoạn 2026–2030 sẽ 
tạo dư địa gia tăng quy mô kinh doanh cho AST, với số điểm kinh doanh dự 
phóng đạt 145 điểm vào cuối 2026 và tăng lên 182 điểm vào năm 2030. Cụ thể: 
- Trong ngắn hạn, tăng trưởng năm 2026 được hỗ trợ bởi việc ghi nhận 
đủ năm doanh thu từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất 
trong năm 2025, cùng 6 điểm mới tại T2 Nội Bài trong năm 2026, trong 
đó có 2 cửa hàng miễn thuế và 2 cửa hàng Memories of Vietnam khai 
trương trong 2Q2026. 
- Trong trung và dài hạn, các dự án Cảng HKQT Long Thành, Cảng HKQT 
Phú Quốc nâng cấp mở rộng và Cảng HKQT Gia Bình sẽ góp phần thúc 
đẩy ngành hàng không Việt Nam nói chung, bổ sung mặt bằng thương 
mại, mở ra cơ hội phát triển các mảng kinh doanh cốt lõi cho AST nói 
riêng bao gồm các mảng bán lẻ, miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood và 
phòng chờ thương gia. Với kinh nghiệm khai thác 141 điểm kinh doanh 
tại 6 sân bay quốc tế và lịch sử trúng thầu tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất, T2 Nội 
Bài trong thời gian qua, chúng tôi kỳ vọng AST có khả năng cạnh tranh 
cao trong việc đấu thầu quyền khai thác mặt bằng tại các sân bay mới, 
đặc biệt là Gia Bình, qua đó duy trì vị thế và thị phần kinh doanh phi 
hàng không tại khu vực phía Bắc khi một phần lưu lượng hành khách 
được phân bổ lại từ Nội Bài.

Cơ cấu kinh doanh và hiệu quả vận 
hành hỗ trợ mở rộng biên lợi nhuận  
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng biên lợi nhuận của AST tiếp tục cải thiện trong 2026 - 2027, 
nhờ định hướng mở rộng các mảng có biên lợi nhuận tốt như hàng miễn thuế 
và phòng chờ thương gia, kết hợp tối ưu hiệu quả vận hành tại các điểm kinh 
doanh. Cụ thể: (1) AST ưu tiên gia tăng số điểm kinh doanh hàng miễn thuế 
nhằm đón đầu tăng trưởng nhanh và ổn định của lượng khách quốc tế, qua đó 
nâng đóng góp lợi nhuận từ hoạt động mua sắm tại sân bay, (2) mở rộng mạng 
lưới phòng chờ, đồng thời gia tăng lượng khách sử dụng trên nền năng lực phục 
vụ hiện hữu và (3) tối ưu hóa hiệu quả hoạt động thông qua cải thiện bố trí mặt 
bằng, danh mục sản phẩm và kiểm soát chi phí tại các điểm kinh doanh, qua 
đó tăng doanh thu và lợi nhuận thu được trên mỗi khách hàng.

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA, giá 
mục tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA đối với cổ phiếu AST, với giá mục tiêu 76,500 
VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương tiềm năng tăng giá 20% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 
21/09/2026, dựa trên triển vọng tăng trưởng từ lượng khách quốc tế, mở rộng 
mạng lưới điểm kinh doanh và khả năng cải thiện biên lợi nhuận của doanh 
nghiệp. Chúng tôi sử dụng phương pháp định giá từng phần (SOTP), bao gồm 
DCF cho hoạt động kinh doanh cốt lõi của AST và P/E mục tiêu 11x đối với phần 
lợi nhuận thuộc sở hữu AST tại VinaCS. Các giả định chính gồm: (1) lượng khách 
quốc tế/nội địa tăng trung bình 11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030, (2) số điểm kinh 
doanh tăng từ 145 điểm cuối 2026 lên 182 điểm năm 2030, với giả định AST 
trúng thầu mặt bằng tại cả Long Thành và Gia Bình và (3) biên lợi nhuận gộp 
2026/2027 đạt 64.7/65.5%. Đối với VinaCS, chúng tôi kỳ vọng nhà máy suất ăn 
Long Thành sẽ hỗ trợ tăng trưởng lợi nhuận trong trung và dài hạn khi dự kiến 
có thể bắt đầu đóng góp lợi nhuận cho AST từ 2028.

Rủi ro đối với định giá bao gồm: (1) AST không giành được quyền khai thác tại 
Long Thành và Gia Bình như kỳ vọng, (2) lưu lượng khách dịch chuyển giữa các 
sân bay mới và hiện hữu khiến doanh thu tại một số điểm kinh doanh bị sụt 
giảm, (3) cạnh tranh gia tăng từ các doanh nghiệp kinh doanh dịch vụ phi hàng 
không khác và (4) căng thẳng địa chính trị kéo dài, giá dầu tăng gây áp lực lên 
hoạt động hàng không và chi tiêu của hành khách.

Document 335: Is FEMO Too Concentrated_

Source PDF: all report pdf/Is FEMO Too Concentrated_.pdf

Date: 2026-10-14

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Is FEMO Too Concentrated?
Date:    29 minutes ago
Is FEMO Too Concentrated?

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Is FEMO Too Concentrated?
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 13 Oct 2026
View in browser
Photo by Michal Balog / Unsplash

I. Stocks: Happy Fourth Anniversary!
Yesterday marked the fourth anniversary of the stock market bull run. The
S&P 500 is up 118.3% since it began. The bull market has survived the
pandemic, global supply-chain disruptions, rebounding inflation, two oil
price shocks, a significant round of Fed rate hikes, soaring bond yields, a
financial crisis, Trump's tariffs, and two wars. It has also survived concerns
about the market’s leadership being too concentrated in a few
outperforming stocks.

### Page 2

Only two of the S&P 500's 11 sectors have outperformed the index:
Information Technology (258.1%) and Communication Services (196.7%).
The others posted reasonably strong bull-market returns. Keep in mind
that the three most underperforming sectors include many companies that
pay relatively high dividends to shareholders (charts).

### Page 3

Since 2025, the current bull market has been increasingly led by FEMO
(Fabulous Earnings Momentum). Since the bull market started, S&P 500
forward earnings is up 73.9%, while the forward P/E is up 25.4% (chart).
A recent concern is that FEMO has been too concentrated. Our diffusion
indexes show that the percentages of S&P 500 companies with positive 3-
month and 12-month increases in forward revenues and forward earnings
have risen significantly during the bull market (charts).

### Page 4

On a y/y basis, all sectors are showing solid gains in forward earnings,
with the three worst laggards including many companies that pay relatively
high dividends to shareholders, as noted above (chart).

### Page 5

Nevertheless, this has been a tough year for stock pickers so far, with
41.2% of S&P 500 companies’ stocks down ytd (chart).
II. Update on the US Economy
As we noted recently, analysts expect positive revenue and earnings
growth across all 11 S&P 500 sectors in Q3 on a y/y basis. This broad-
based strength reflects the remarkable resilience of the US economy. The
latest economic indicators provide further evidence of that resilience.
Here's more:

(1) Bank earnings. The big banks kicked off Q3 earnings season today,
with JPMorgan Chase, Goldman Sachs, Citigroup, and Wells Fargo
reporting solid results. Their commentary confirms that strong consumer
spending and business investment are translating to robust lending growth

### Page 6

and investment banking activity, while credit quality remains healthy
(chart).
JPMorgan CEO Jamie Dimon pointed to "strong business investment and
ongoing consumer spending," while Goldman Sachs CEO David Solomon
observed that "the economy continues to be very, very resilient despite
some of the headwinds." Wells Fargo CEO Charlie Scharf offered a
particularly upbeat assessment, noting that despite mixed consumer
sentiment, "consumers are spending more, loan delinquencies are low,

and deposit and investment balances have grown." He added that strong
employment, wage growth, and AI infrastructure investment continue to
outweigh the risks from higher interest rates, oil prices, and inflation.
Citigroup CEO Jane Fraser likewise observed that despite all the attention
surrounding the AI boom, "the consumer is still driving much of the
growth."
(2) Consumer spending. The latest Redbook data confirm the bank
CEOs’ upbeat assessments of the US consumer. Redbook same-store

sales surged 10.4% y/y during the week of October 9, the strongest
increase since 2022 (chart).

### Page 7

Bank of America's September Consumer Checkpoint provides further
evidence of consumer strength. Credit and debit card spending per
household rose 6.2% y/y, or 5.1% excluding gasoline, both the third-
fastest rates in four years! Even more encouraging, spending growth is
broadening across income groups. Card spending rose 5.5% y/y among
lower-income households, compared with 4.9% and 4.8% among middle-
and higher-income households, respectively. Only the top 5% of earners

posted stronger growth, at 5.9%.
(3) Labor market. What's behind the broadening of consumer spending?
A key pillar is the labor market. Bank of America also reports that after-tax
wage and salary growth reached 4.5% y/y among lower-income
households in September, compared with 3.8% and 3.7% among middle-
and higher-income households, respectively. Stronger wage growth is
helping consumers absorb higher living costs without sacrificing
discretionary spending.

The latest weekly ADP data suggest that labor market strength persists.
Private-sector employment rose by 24,750 during the week of September
26, the sixth consecutive weekly acceleration and the strongest gain since
early June (chart). That implies a monthly pace of roughly 96,300 jobs,
comfortably above most estimates of the breakeven pace needed to keep
the unemployment rate low and stable.

### Page 8

Meanwhile, small business hiring plans moderated somewhat in
September but remained above historical norms. The latest NFIB survey
showed that 17% of small businesses planned to increase employment,
well above the historical average of 11% (chart). Job openings remained 8
percentage points above their historical average. A shortage of qualified
workers continues to constrain hiring, with 45% of firms reporting no
qualified applicants for their open positions compared with the historical

average of 37%.
The bad news is that the NFIB survey shows that pricing pressures
remained elevated in September (chart).

### Page 9

The most frequently cited problem faced by small business owners is the
quality of labor (chart). We expect they will solve this problem by boosting
productivity through AI and other technologies.
💡
Join the discussion with Ed below! To leave comments or questions,
log in to the Yardeni QuickTakes website and post them at the end
of the QuickTakes article. Paid members' contributions may be
featured in our segment, "Ed Answers Your Questions".

### Page 10

clause.

► 博通3Q26營收達 295.9億美元 ，年增86%，
優 於 市 場 預 期。AI 半 導 體 營 收 達167 億美
元，年增221%，占整體營收比重逾五成。然
而，短期股價表現卻趨向震盪 ，主因為4Q26
營收與營業利益率指引未大幅超越已偏高的
市場預期。
► 觀察公司目前的Google TPU、Meta MTIA、
OpenAI與Anthropic 專案仍持續推進 ，博通
訂單需求未見轉弱 。公司營收成長主要來自
客製化XPU與AI networking，管理層預期未
來數年AI網路產品以及光通訊相關產品的營
收成長速度可與 XPU相當，顯示AI營收成長
並非完全依賴單一 ASIC專案，將有望支持營
收能見度。
► 整體而言，本次財報訊號偏正向，從未來2年
的AI營收展望來看 ，除大型CSP與模型業者
對 客 製 化AI 晶 片 的 投 資 未 見 放 緩， 在 AI
networking產品的營收成長加速下 ，料將進
一步提升博通在AI叢集中的內含價值。

博通財報接力AI浪潮，客製化晶片與AI網路支撐營收能見度擴張

### Page 6

6
投資焦點金融股
► 隨著AI資本支出繼續創高 ，整體AI產業的自
由現金流卻面臨更大壓力 。自2022年底AI資
本支出週期啟動以來 ，獲利與現金流的分歧
便日趨明顯 ，截至2Q26，4大CSP過去12個
月的淨利為5,990億美元，而自由現金流僅剩
1,690億美元。
► AI資料中心持續建設 ，最新統計包括亞馬遜
等AI資料中心營運商 相關企業 發債量2026年
至今已較去年 增加約98%。代表在企業自由
現金流持續放緩的背景下 ，AI企業的對外融
資需求已快速擴增 ，並同步挹注了金融業的
手續費與交易收入。
► 隨著投銀業務 、股債交易及財富 管理業務表
現強勁 ，2Q26金融 股財報普遍優於 市場 預
期，故市場紛紛上修對於金融股的獲利成長
預估。此外，在年底前聯準會仍具升息1碼空
間，加上殖利率環境維持高檔的環境下 ，亦
有利金融股延續基本面成長趨勢 ，並有望持
續獲得市場資金青睞。
對等關稅宣布後，內需防禦股獲利下修幅度最少 金融股獲利成長持續上修4大CSP淨利與自由現金流呈現分岐，發債融資擴增

AI資金需求擴增助力金融產業獲利成長

### Page 7

投資焦點工業股
► 2026年第2季財報數據顯示 ，4大CSP之AI相
關資本支出持續增加，並同步提高未來2年的
財務指引 。目前市場普遍預期 ，今年底AI相
關資本支出將達到約 9,070億美元 ，2027年
底更有望超過1.2兆美元。反映AI算力需求迅
速攀升 ，相關基礎設施需求遠超供給 。過去
數季，大型CSP企業幾乎每季皆調高資本支
出預期 ，推動產業持續進行 「AI軍備競賽 」
以確保未來競爭優勢。
► 觀察最新8月ISM製造業指數雖較上個月略為
下滑，但新訂單 、新出口訂單僅小幅回落 ，
整體指數仍維持在 50以上的擴張區間 ，顯示
製造業動能仍保持強勁 。加上耐久財訂單年
增率亦維持高檔 ，代表AI資本支出擴增趨勢
帶來的外溢效應未有改變。
► 因此，市場普遍預估 2026年工業股獲利成長
將持續攀升 ，除了反映資本支出擴大外 ，地
緣政治反覆亦將加大 國防工業需求 ，令整體
工業類股後續有望獲得更多資金進駐。
對等關稅宣布後，內需防禦股獲利下修幅度最少 工業獲利成長強勁攀升AI資本支出快速擴增，推升製造業相關數據動能

AI資本支出續增帶旺工業獲利動能

### Page 8

8
股市
 全球主要國家債務攀升 ，利率創高添股市評價修正風險 ，加上科技股舉債投入資本支出 ，未來獲利
與變現能力備受關注 。美伊衝突加劇不確定性 ，短線可透過布局醫療與公用事業等防禦 股，降低市
場震盪風險 ，科技及AI半導體等發展趨勢不變 ，獲利等基本面支持長線發展 ，採逢回分批布局 ，並
分散國家與產業配置。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，全球利率彈升有利銀行股 ，歐、日銀行股基本面佳 ，日本半導體股
分批布局較穩健，歐洲國防軍工仍具投資吸引力。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，中長
期看好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分
可關注金融、工業等產業機會
區域：日本銀行股、日本半導體股、歐洲
金融、國防
債市
 美國30年期公債殖利率維持相對高檔 ，應避免超長天期債券投資 ，預期年底前美國10年期公債利率
將於4.5%-5.0%震盪，可逢利率走升時 ，以中短天期債券進行鎖利領息 。高信評債體質 較佳，具穩
定現金流與還款能力 ，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸引力者 ，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業 等產業。

 可以非美貨幣債券如歐元或澳幣計價投資級債券，分散單一貨幣風險。
債種：中天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金融、
科技、通訊及公用事業等
衛星配置：非美貨幣債券分散風險
匯市
 聯準會9月升息預期回升，美元短線高檔震盪。
 日本銀行釋出鷹派訊號，9月升息可能性升高，有利日圓止貶回升。
日圓震盪偏升
商品
 中東衝突反覆 ，荷姆茲海峽短期恢復正常通行機率偏低 ，加上石油庫存探低 ，預期今年能源仍呈供
不應求態勢，油價維持偏高水位。
 美國財務壓力與去美元化之兩個長期結構性問題依然存在，央行買盤提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
短期黃金區間操作，中長期維持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

Document 359: Persistent energy pressures have further to run (2026-10-19)

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
MIDDLE EAST
BULLETIN
Persistent energy pressures have further to run
19 October 2026
Key points
• Shipping disruptions, depleted
inventories and elevated freight
costs are keeping physical crude
and fuel markets tight despite a

reported recovery in Gulf exports.
• Brent is forecast to average
US$101/bbl in Q4 before easing
to US$77/bbl in Q4 2027.
• Refined product markets are
expected to ease more slowly than
crude, with elevated gasoil cracks,
shipping and insurance costs slowing
declines in petrol and diesel prices.
• Under an escalation scenario,
renewed conflict and deeper supply
disruptions could push Brent towards
US$150/bbl in H1 2027, prolonging
elevated fuel and LNG prices.

• Under an early resolution scenario, a
durable US-Iran agreement accelerates
supply recovery, taking Brent towards
US$65/bbl by Q4 2027 and bringing
fuel and LNG prices down faster.

Brent crude
US$101/bbl

### Page 2

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Low inventories and higher shipping costs will keep oil and
fuel prices elevated, with Brent futures forecast to average
US$101/bbl in Q4 before easing to US$77/bbl by Q4 2027
as supply recovers. Refined fuels and LNG prices should

ease more slowly. Risks are two-sided: escalation could
push Brent towards US$150/bbl, while an early US-Iran
agreement could see a faster normalisation in supply with
prices returning close to pre-conflict levels by end-2027.
This article was first published in the October Market Outlook .
Brent crude is back above US$100/bbl on fading confidence
in a lasting US-Iran agreement and intermittent attacks on
shipping. Buffers remain depleted, with the IEA estimating

global observed inventories were 507mb lower at the end of
August than at the start of the conflict.
This comes despite reported crude exports through the Strait of
Hormuz recovering to around 70% to 90% of pre-conflict levels
in late September. Saudi and UAE exports through the Yanbu and
Fujairah pipelines continue, with the East-West pipeline back to
normal after the early September attacks. Tanker crossings have
risen, although transponders remain switched off, leaving visible

transit at 1% to 4% of pre-conflict levels, while cargo transfers in
the Gulf of Oman have increased as an alternative route.
However, these workarounds are slower and more costly. Ship-
to-ship transfers can take 5 to 10 days, US escorts through
the Gulf via a route close to the Oman shoreline cause delays
while some tankers are taking longer routes. Fewer ships are
also available, with many redeployed to other markets. Higher
insurance premiums are adding to costs, with Gulf-to-China

tanker rates above US$0.9mn a day, around 15x higher than a
year ago. With prompt barrels (available for physical delivery)
scarce, the gap between physical and futures prices widened
again, with Dated Brent around US$136/bbl in early October
against US$101/bbl for front-month futures.
Oil and fuel prices push higher ...
Refined prices have risen more than crude
Refined product markets were already tight going into the
conflict. Reduced Gulf supply, exacerbated by Ukrainian strikes

on Russian refineries, has added to that tightness. Together
with higher shipping costs and rising Dated Brent prices,
Singapore Gasoil 10ppm (diesel) has roughly doubled since the
Strait closed, compared with around 40% for crude futures.
Consequently, Singapore gasoil crack spread widened
to an average of around US$36.1/bbl in early October,
from US$17.5/bbl across 2025. Alongside the Australian
government’s excise tax returning to normal, after it had been

halved, this has lifted national diesel and petrol prices to
around A$2.83/L and A$2.41/L.
Outlook
We do not see a lasting US-Iran agreement until at least
mid-2027. In the meantime, we assume there will not be a
return to full-scale military intervention. However, periodic
Iranian attacks on ships and facilities are expected to continue,
as will the US blockade of Iranian exports, keeping a supply
risk premium in place. We look for Brent futures to average

around US$101/bbl in Q4, then ease gradually as demand
from refiners and governments to rebuild stocks partly offsets
recovering supply. As the premium fades and the market
moves into surplus in late 2027, we forecast Brent at around
US$77/bbl in Q4 2027, easing towards US$70/bbl in 2028 as
Middle East production fully recovers.
Oil balance to gradually improve over 2027
Global oil supply is expected to rise by 4.0mb/d (4.0%) to
103.7mb/d in 2027, following a 6.1% fall in 2026. Most of

this reflects a gradual recovery in Gulf output, with Houthi
Sian Fenner, Head of Business & Industry Economics
Justin Smirk, Senior Economist
Luka Belobajdic, Economist

### Page 3

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
attacks on Red Sea shipping keeping Saudi production below
its pre-conflict level until H2 2027. Meanwhile, we look for UAE
production to continue to improve over 2027 and lift above
pre-conflict levels from 2028, as it is no longer constrained by

OPEC quotas. We do not expect a meaningful return of Iranian
oil until an agreement is reached, and damage sustained during
the conflict means production will take time to recover.
We expect global oil demand to be 103.7mb/d in 2026, 0.8mb/d
(0.7%) higher than in 2025, and to rise a further 1.9mb/d (1.8%)
to 105.6mb/d in 2027. Chinese crude imports will continue to
recover over the coming months as refiners restock ahead of
Lunar New Year, but we expect only a gradual return to pre-

conflict levels, as the electrification of transport limits the
recovery in Chinese demand. Globally, demand for diesel and jet
fuel recovers as transport and aviation activity picks up, although
some demand destruction and efficiency gains are expected
to be permanent. The conflict has also highlighted strategic
vulnerabilities, particularly across Asia, so we expect a push to
rebuild reserves adding around 0.5% to demand over 2027.
Fuel prices to ease more slowly than crude

Refined product supply is expected to recover more slowly
than crude, with Gulf refinery runs not returning to pre-war
levels until at least late 2027. Outside the Gulf, there has
been some constructive news. The US has lifted sanctions
on Russian diesel until April 2027, with Russia saying it will
supply more than 4Mt over the coming months, while China
has lifted its ban on refined product exports. However, we
are cautious about how much this will add to the market in

the short term. Much of the Russian supply is headed to the
US and will depend on Russia’s ability to restore refining and
export capacity, while doubts remain over the Russia-Ukraine
energy ceasefire announced by President Trump. China still
manages exports through quotas, which are likely to increase
only gradually as domestic stocks are rebuilt. Refinery runs
should lift ahead of Lunar New Year, but any increase in
exports will take time to reach regional markets.

With product supply recovering slowly and depleted inventories
being rebuilt, Singapore gasoil crack is likely to stay above its
long-run average even as Brent eases, narrowing from US$71/
bbl in Q4 to US$27/bbl by Q4 2027. With Singapore Gas 10ppm
prices, shipping and insurance costs to remain elevated and
easing only gradually in 2027, we forecast Australian diesel
to retail at an average of 274¢/L in Q4, easing to 200¢/L in Q4
2027, and petrol at 230¢/L and 187¢/L respectively.

LNG prices supported through 2027
Japanese LNG prices surged 15.2%mth in September,
averaging US$25.4/MMBtu as supply disruptions in Qatar
and its inability to divert exports around the Strait of Hormuz
tightened markets and intensified competition between
importers. While prices are now more than double pre-conflict
levels, demand destruction across Asia is providing some
offset to tight supply
Our Q4 forecast for Japanese LNG is now US$24.5/mmbtu and

we expect the market to remain structurally tight, with supply
recovery likely to be gradual and prices not returning to pre-
conflict levels until around 2028. Still, Australia’s domestic
gas market should remain insulated, supported by the FY2027
Federal Budget’s domestic gas reservation scheme, which
requires exporters to supply the domestic market with the
equivalent of up to 20% of gas exports. So far this year,
despite the increase in LNG spot prices in Asia, Australian

natural gas prices have remained under the $12 per GJ cap
that has proposed by the Commonwealth government. We are
expecting this to continue well into 2027.
Risks around our oil price outlook are two-sided
Talks are still ongoing in the background, with the US and
Iran exchanging proposals to reopen the Strait, but the gap
between them remains wide. The conflict has also expanded
to the Red Sea and, with inventories sharply drawn down,
there is a smaller buffer to absorb a further shock.

Using the Oxford Economics model, we assess an escalation
scenario, where energy markets face a deeper and more
persistent disruption as the conflict in the Middle East
intensifies. In this case, Brent futures rise to around US$150/
bbl in H1 2027, only dipping to below US$100/bbl in Q2 2028.
We also consider an early resolution scenario, where shipping
and Gulf supply normalise faster, returning Brent to near pre-
conflict levels by end-2027.
Escalation scenario

In the escalation scenario we assume:
Talks stall and the conflict escalates:  the gap between the
US and Iran proves too wide and the US resumes strikes after
the midterms. Iran responds with attacks on shipping and Gulf
energy infrastructure, damaging export, storage and refining
facilities. A lasting agreement is not reached until 2028.
Export routes are disrupted: more frequent attacks curtail
dark crossings of the Strait and ship-to-ship transfers in the

Gulf of Oman. Houthi attacks also cut off Saudi alternative
routes, forcing Gulf producers to cut output and Middle
East crude exports sharply lower. New routes and shipping
arrangements eventually support a recovery in Gulf flows, but
trade patterns take time to adjust.
... and risks remain and while they are two sided ...

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
4
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
Product supply fails to recover:  The Russia–Ukraine energy
ceasefire does not hold and strikes on Russian refineries
continue, limiting how much diesel Russia can export,
even with the US sanctions waiver extended. China also

reintroduces its export ban to protect domestic supply.
Oil prices lift to around US$150/bbl in H1 2027: With little
buffer to absorb the supply loss, competition for physical
delivery intensifies, with physical prices well above futures
as prompt barrels remain scarce. Higher prices support
supply growth outside OPEC and drive demand destruction,
alongside further emergency stock releases, but a deeper
and more prolonged global deficit remains. Brent stays above

US$100/bbl until mid-2028 as governments rebuild reserves.
The Singapore gasoil crack widens and, with freight and
insurance costs also higher, Australian diesel and petrol prices
average around 247 cpl and 231 cpl at end 2027. Japanese
LNG also remains elevated around US$23.2/MMBtu in Q4
2027, $9.1 above our baseline.
Early resolution scenario
In the early resolution scenario we assume:
A durable agreement: The US and Iran agree terms to reopen
the Strait by the end of year, and, unlike the June interim deal,

the agreement holds, with progress towards a permanent
settlement in early 2027. Attacks on shipping cease and the
US blockade is lifted, allowing Iranian oil back into the market,
initially from storage. Vessels are quickly repositioned to the
Gulf, with freight and war-risk insurance easing from early
2027 rather than mid-year.
A faster recovery in supply:  With export routes secure, Gulf
production and damaged refinery and export capacity recover

sooner than in the baseline. Outside the Gulf, the US extends
its waiver on Russian diesel beyond April 2027 and China
raises export quotas as domestic stocks are rebuilt.
Demand settles below pre-conflict trend: Some demand
destruction and efficiency gains persist, particularly in China.
With inventories rebuilding faster, government restocking is
smaller and more gradual than in the baseline.
Prices fall quickly, but restocking provides a floor:  Brent

Brent futures ease to around US$88/bbl in Q1 2027 as the
premium for prompt physical barrels unwinds. As the market
moves into balance, Brent eases to around US$65/bbl by Q4
2027, close to pre-conflict levels.
Refined product prices ease faster on lower freight costs with
the Singapore gasoil crack narrowing. Australian diesel and
petrol prices fall to around 182 cpl and 170 cpl by Q4 2027. Gas
markets also ease, although damage to Qatar’s gas facility means

export capacity recovers gradually. Japanese LNG falls to around
US$11.9/MMBtu by end-2027, $2.1 below our baseline.
Economic impact
In the escalation scenario, higher fuel costs flow through to
transport, food and other energy-intensive goods such as
plastics and fertilisers. Australian headline CPI remains above
4.5% for much of 2027, with underlying inflation remaining
sticky at above 3% until late 2028. The RBA faces a difficult
trade off between much higher inflation and a significant

hit to activity, as households cut discretionary spending and
businesses defer investment. Higher LNG export earnings
provide only a moderate offset. In the early resolution scenario,
headline inflation is around 0.1ppt below the baseline in 2027.
New Zealand is more exposed in both scenarios, with food
and energy a larger share of its CPI and no offsetting energy
exports. Headline inflation is around 1ppt higher in the
escalation scenario in Q1 2027.

... the upside presents a more dramatic shift in prices

Sales Trader Commentary: TAIEX 49k Wobble -
July De Ja Vu, or Just Pausing Before the Santa
Rally? | GSTW
13 October 2026 | 4:11 PM Taiwan Standard Time
Robert Wang
Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C. Taipei Branch
FICC & Equities
Index lost the 49k points threshold and saw the biggest decline on a point basis since
the end of July. Market seemed to lose the steam to break through 50k points after
the past choppy sessions. Is this July de ja vu or some healthy pullback? Foreigners

and domestic investors seem to share different narrative. Check out below for our
investor feedback.
Key Investor Feedback and Observations
Positioning. we saw meaningful outflows today from all kinds of investors today,
again focusing in ex-TSMC large caps. Today foreigners net sold US$5.2bn today,
the second largest in the history (simultaneously drove TWD weakness over 32.23
today). Domestic investors, especially retail, on the other hand, anchored the

market with the record high margin loan balance (as of 10/12/2026). Local funds
remained buyer as well mainly thanks to ETFs inflow.
Macro. CPI headline brought uncertainty and forced global investors to turn
cautious, which was exacerbated by high yields and crude. For Taiwan, AI trade is
relatively resilient to the Middle Eastern tension and high energy prices. However,
at some point, it is going to have a real economic impact either through Fed or

some other macro data.
Sentiment. Surprisingly, people are still bullish in Taiwan, though they also
acknowledge some choppy trading sessions ahead. Local investor feedback is not
calling for capitulation; in fact, most believe this is just another "two-steps forward
one-step backward" cadence towards another record high. Nonetheless, some
retail start taking chips off the table. This echoes our observation among the
foreign investors. Yes, the AI fundamental future is all sunshine, but as we head into

the end of year, it is natural for them to protect their great fun thus far this year
instead of adding on immediate risk. I'd imagine the next few weeks are going to be
full of 1) rapid sector rotation; 2) weak or unsustainable momentum before Santa
comes into town after the midterm election for our year-end rally Christmas gift.
TSMC Earnings and Others. We know the numbers are going to be good, and we
also know people are going to sell-on-facts after TSMC earnings call; recall most

recent one happened right after the meeting that scarred a lot of investors since.
Perhaps this time people started trimming ahead (and maybe too early?), which
provides some window for a quick rebound into small and mid cap. OCP summit is
happening right now, and TPCA Show will start next week. Watch out for single
stock movement mentioned in the conferences.

Sector. 1) ABF/CCL is still the crowded long sectors for both foreign and domestic
investor, but echoing on above points, investors need more catalysts to add on
risks or drive the re-ratings. 2) Power is another big issues that data centers need
to resolve, power solutions and fuel cells name are also key focus in Taiwan. 3) Let's
not forget about OSATs. We need more equipment and testing capacity to suffice
enlarging demand from both ASIC and GPU.

Key Stocks I Follow: Unimicron, Kinsus, NYPCB, ZDT, EMC, TUC, Delta, Lite-On,
Kaori, ASE, KYEC, All Ring
Risk. Upside looks thin in the short term. Good earnings are not enough - look at
Nanya Tech and Memory sector today. Chasing momentum now may get hurt. I also
smelled some short unwind today (i.e. funding short names outperformance such
as Realtek, Novatek, Shipper, etc) - signs of de-leveraging. Directionless is how I
would describe the past few days, but maybe it's time to find some value Buy?

realize each day a loss or a gain which is a multiple (for leveraged ETFs and ETNs) or the inverse (for inverse ETFs and ETNs) of the

performance on that day of their underlying index or benchmark. For this reason, if they are held for a period longer than one day, their
performance over such longer periods of time can differ significantly from the stated multiple of the performance (or the inverse of the
performance) of their underlying index or benchmark during the same period of time. This effect can be magnified in volatile markets.
Prior to entering into a transaction in leveraged or inverse ETFs, you should be aware of the general risks associated with such

transactions. You should not enter into leveraged or inverse ETFs transactions unless you understand the nature and extent of your risk
exposure. You should also be satisfied that the leveraged or inverse ETFs transaction is appropriate for you in light of your circumstances
and financial condition. ETFs and ETNs that are linked to commodity futures do not offer direct exposure to the commodity's spot price,
and may perform differently than the spot price for the commodity itself. Performance differential can be magnified if a specific

condition persists in the market for a commodity that creates a disparity between near-term future prices and long-term future prices
and may lead to unexpected performance results. Other factors, such as roll yield, transaction costs, management fees, and taxes may
cause deviation in performance between the spot price of a commodity and commodity futures. You should not assume that an ETF or
ETN that is linked to commodity futures will provide an effective hedge because of a negative correlation with equities or other asset

swaps, and other derivatives as well as non-investment-grade securities - give rise to substantial risk and are not available to nor suitable
for all investors. If you have any questions about whether you are eligible to enter into these transactions with Goldman Sachs, please
contact your sales representative. Foreign-currency-denominated securities are subject to fluctuations in exchange rates that could have

values of which are influenced by foreign currencies, effectively assume currency risk.

### Page 2

UNITED STATES 
The US economy is expected to grow above its potential in 2026, with an average annual growth rate of 2. 2%, close to the 2025 rate (2.1%) . 
This apparent resilience to the uncertainty, tariff and energy shocks, together with above-trend productivity growth, masks K-shaped growth, 
driven by investment linked to AI-optimism and consumption by the wealthiest amid a wealth effect (historically high stock market valuations). 
Inflation overshooting is set to continue (3.4% in 2026) at least through 2028, largely because of the rise in oil prices and tariffs – although the 
impact of these latter appears to be less significant than expected. The labour market is showing clear signs of improvement that should bring 
the unemployment rate down toward 4.0%. Given this shift in price and employment risks amid dynamic growth, the FOMC proceeded with a 
first rate hike at September’s meeting and we expect two further 25bp increases in December 2026 and January 2027, bringing the Fed Funds 
target range at 4,25% - 4,50%.

CHINA 
Economic growth slowed to 4.3% y/y in Q2 2026 vs. 5.0% in Q1. It is projected to reach 4.6% in 2026 as a whole, down from 5% in 2025. Growth 
remains characterized by a K-shaped trajectory, and the gap between the strong performance of exports and the fragility of sectors that rely 
on domestic demand has widened this year. Domestic demand has weakened significantly in recent months. Retail sales have barely increased, 
the crisis in the property sector has continued and household confidence remains low. Fiscal policy measures aimed at supporting investment 
and private consumption will increase over the last months of the year . CPI inflation has rebounded slightly since last March, mostly due to 
the rise in global energy prices, as well as due to the increase in electronic good prices and anti-involution measures implemented by the 
authorities. Deflationary pressures persist, however, given weakness in domestic demand.

EUROZONE 
Eurozone growth is expected to slow to 1.1% in 2026 (from 1.3% in 2025), before strengthening to 1.5% in 2027, supported by higher defence 
and infrastructure spending as well as the global AI capex cycle. The economy has proven resilient to the energy shock, notab ly thanks to the 
increasingly tangible impact of G erman defence spending on the real economy. Elevated energy prices in the autumn (especially for natural 
gas), combined with resilient growth, are set to accelerate inflation to a peak around the turn of the year. Annual average i nflation is thus 
expected to reach  3.1% in 2026 (from 2.1% in 2025), before easing to 3.0% in 2027. Given persistent inflation and resilient growth, we 
anticipate another 25bp hike from the ECB in December 2026, bringing the deposit rate to 2.75%, followed by an extended pause . That said, 
stronger than expected second-round effects could lead to more tightening than our current baseline.

### Page 3

FRANCE 
GDP growth remained low in Q2 (0% after -0.2% in Q1) as a result of deteriorated agricultural and construction output, as well as higher 
inflation (2.5% in 2026 according to our forecast, after 0.9% in 2025) . In 2026, GDP growth should  reach 0.5% (after 0.9% in 2025), driven 
mainly by exports.

UNITED KINGDOM 
UK GDP growth would improve slightly to 1.3% in 2026 and remain at this level in 2027  (1.2% in 2025). The economy has so far weathered 
both the energy price shock and domestic political volatility. That said, growth is expected to decelerate in the second half  of the year, while 
inflation is set to remain stubbornly above target (averaging 3.2% in both 2026 and 2027), driven by higher energy prices and mounting second-
round effects. In response, we expect the BoE to deliver a single insurance hike in November 2026, bringing the Bank Rate to 4.00%. The BoE 
is then expected to resume easing in H2 2027, with two cuts taking the Bank Rate to 3.50% by end-year.

JAPAN 
Following 1.2% in 2025, Japan's GDP growth is expected to decelerate to 1.0% in 2026, before rebounding to 1.2% in 2027. Higher inflation 
and production costs associated with the energy shock are expected to weigh on the economy’s overall performance. On the other hand, the 
latter is supported by fiscal policy  and AI-related investment. Inflation has generally overshot the 2% y/y target since 2022. Accordingly, the 
Bank of Japan initiated a cautious process of “adjustment in the degree of monetary accommodation” in 2024, lifting the policy rate to 1.25% 
so far (previously negative) – the highest since 1995. We expect two more hikes by the end of Q1 2027, followed by a return to a more cautious 
pace until the terminal rate of +2.5% is reached in H2 2028. Japan is facing long-term rates pressure, illustrated by historically high 10- and 30-
year yields, probably fueled by the level of public debt and the pace of monetary adjustment.

EXCHANGE RATES 
We expect Fed hikes to temporarily offset structural headwinds and support the dollar. EUR/USD would target 1.14 by end -2026, before the 
dollar's structural downtrend resumes toward 1.20 by end-2027, although political and fiscal risk may weigh on the euro next year. The USD/JPY 
appreciation would continue but at a more moderate pace, with the BoJ still behind the curve despite its hikes. By contrast, GBP/USD would 
see relative support underpinned by a modest fiscal risk premium under the new Labour government, and resilient growth.

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

Monetary Authority of Singapore (MAS)

We expect another slight increase in the 
SGD NEER policy band slope at the MAS’ 
October review, with no change to the 
band’s width or centre. Import cost 
pressures and resilient growth favour a 
calibrated tightening, rather than a more 
forceful adjustment, in line with the 
global monetary policy stance.

Also due to be released alongside the 
MAS’ policy decision are the 3Q26 
advance GDP estimates, which we 
expect to show another quarter of strong 
growth at 5.4% yoy (1.4% qoq sa), 
compared with 5.9% yoy (1.4% qoq sa) in 
2Q26. Robust economic performance 
continued to be driven by artificial 
intelligence ( AI)-led trade activity, 
supporting the manufacturing and 
wholesale trade sectors, while strength 
in the financial sector underpinned the 
resilience of the modern services cluster. 
This came despite a moderation of the 
transport & storage sector, and 
weakness in consumer -facing food 
services and retail sectors.

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Malaysia

We expect Malaysia’s advance GDP 
estimate for 3Q26 to register above -
trend growth of 5.0% yoy, albeit 
moderating from 6.0% yoy  in 2Q26. The 
overall expansion continued to be 
supported by ongoing strength in the 
export-oriented manufacturing sector, 
underpinned by strong  AI-related 
demand for electronics, and sustained 
domestic demand, backed by healthy 
investment and private consumption 
trends. However, contraction in mining 
and weak agricultural activity dragged 
overall growth.

Singapore

We expect firm Singapore non -oil 
domestic exports (NODX) growth of 28.0% 
yoy in September 2026, although 
moderating from August’s exceptional 
surge of 46.2%. Although favourable 
base effects largely faded in September, 
electronics shipments likely remained 
the primary growth driver, underpinned 
by robust AI -related demand amid 
sustained global investment in AI 
infrastructure, while non-electronics 
exports were uneven.

### Page 3

The Week Ahead October 9, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         3

China

Exports growth is expected to remain 
robust at 25.7% y oy in September, 
supported by continued strength in 
electronics demand. Both the official and 
Caixin manufacturing PMIs rose above 
the 50 expansion threshold during the 
month, pointing to improving 
manufacturing activity. External trade 
indicators also stren gthened, with 
average daily deadweight tonnage 
handled at China's 20 major ports 
accelerating from -0.7% yoy in August to 
7.2% in September. Freight activity 
improved as well, as growth in 
international cargo flights picked up 
from -1.4% y oy to 3.6% over the same 
period.

On the price front, factory -gate and raw 
material prices rebounded amid the 
renewed escalation in geopolitical 
tensions. Main raw material purchasing 
price and producer price subPMI 
increased from 56.6 and 50.4 to 60.8 and 
54.0 during the period. However, 
downstream consumer price pressures 
are likely to remain relatively subdued 
under the pricing-band mechanism. As a 
result, CPI inflation is expected to edge 
up from 0.8% y oy in August to 1.0% in 
September. Nevertheless, soft domestic 
demand is expected to continue 
constraining the pace of consumer price 
increases.

India

September inflation is likely to gather 
momentum to 5.7% y/y, partly driven by 
base effects, from 4.8% the month 
before. Food segments continue to 
register gains, with pressures 
broadening to include perishables, 
edible oils, rice, pulses, sugar, etc., 
according to high-frequency data. Under 
non-food, retail pump prices have not 
been adjusted further, though other fuel 
products, such as non -subsidised LPG, 
diesel and ATF (still up by double digits 
y/y, though slower than in August), are 
up on the year. Core  inflation is also 
likely to tick up, aligning with the central 
bank's view that price risks are no longer 
benign. Pressures have built up due to 
the overhang of an uneven and sub -par 
monsoon, drought conditions in parts of 
the country, and elevated oil pr ices. In 
the face of higher headline prints, we 
expect the RBI to keep the door open for 
another rate hike in December 2026.

高利率環境不利於公用事業評價
分析師／謝孟璇
研究範疇／公用事業/汽車/科技
熱門議題 / 投資關鍵
《市場預估聯準會年底再升息一碼 ，不利公用事業
獲利與評價》
聯準會在 9月FOMC會議決議升息一碼 ，使美國目
標 利 率 區 間 上 升 至3.75~4% ， 且 經 濟 預 估 摘 要
( SEP )顯示年底前將再升息一碼；另外，受累於近
期美伊陷入外交僵局 ，投資者對於衝突長期化的擔
憂推升了國際油價 ，導致做為全球無風險利率定價
基準的10年期美債殖利率攀升至5.2%附近。公用事
業屬於資本密集型產業 ，極度依賴債務融資來支持
基礎設施建設與更新 ，升息提高了企業利息支出 、
直接壓縮利潤空間 ，對公用事業獲利與產業評價相

對不利。此外，過去兩、三年市場為電力公用事業
公司注入AI溢價，導致板塊估值偏高 ，預估短期內
公用事業股價大幅向上的機率較低 。紐約州、德州、
賓州相繼對大型資料中心新建案祭出暫停令或更嚴
格的電網接入限制 ，部分對資料中心建設較保守州
的公用事業公司長期營收成長可能減緩。

紀彥呈 / 資訊軟體、半導體  
+886-2-2326-9899#1940 / ianchi@cathayfut.com.tw

台積電 2330 TT  ( 電子–半導體 ) 
積厚成勢，晶耀巔峰 
投資與評價 
1. 台積電 3Q26 受惠 AI GPU、ASIC、CPU 及網通晶片需求強勁，HPC 營收
季增 22%，N2 營收占比提升至 8%，營運創新高；4Q26 隨 N2 於新竹及
高雄持續快速放量，N3 維持高稼動率，營運續創歷史新高。 
2. 台積電 26 年受惠 AI/HPC 需求強勁，N3 維持高稼動 、N2 加速放量，A16
於 2H26 量產，CoWoS 產能持續擴充，預估 EPS 110.83 元；預估 27 年
N2 年產能達 2.4 倍，台灣南科及美國亞利桑那 N3 新廠陸續量產，AI 加
速器與 Agentic AI 需求續增，EPS 151.11 元。 
3. 目前台積電股價交易在 2,440 元(16.1X 27(F) PER)，位於近三年歷史本益
比參考區間 12~26x 中間偏下位置，因 2 奈米量產爬坡以及海外廠折舊稀
釋，27 年毛利率將小幅衰退，本益比由中間偏上之 23 倍下調至 22 倍，
因調高 27 年獲利預估，故維持強力買進投資建議，目標價由3,220 元(23 
x 修正前 FY27E EPS)調升至 3,320 元(22 x 修正後 FY27E EPS)。 
重點更新 
台積電 3Q26 受惠 AI 及先進製程需求強勁，優於預期且創歷史新高 
N2 製程自 4Q25 量產後持續擴大產能，帶動 3Q26 HPC 與智慧型手機營收分
別季增 22%及 10%，占整體營收比重達 68%及 21%。在先進製程占比提升及
晶圓出貨量增加帶動下，營收 1.494 兆元(+17.6% QoQ) 。雖然 N3 產能利用
率維持高檔，加上整體營運規模持續擴大，惟 N2 初期量產仍面臨較高的製造
成 本 與 折 舊 負 擔 ， 形 成 稀 釋 ， 但 稀 釋 程 度 低 於 我 們 原 先 預 期 ， 毛 利 率
66.4%(-1.3ppts QoQ) ，營業利益 8,764.45 億元(+15.1% QoQ) ，稅後淨利
7,633.41 億元(+8.0% QoQ)，EPS 29.43 元，優於預期且創歷史新高。  
預期台積電 4Q26 受惠 AI 需求持續擴張，N2 放量推升營運再創新高 
隨 CSP 持續增加 AI 基礎建設投資，AI 加速器、Agentic AI 相關 CPU 及網通
晶片對先進製程的需求持續增加，上修 4Q26 營收預估 9.5%至 1.699 兆元
(+13.8% QoQ)。N2 處於快速擴產階段，初期量產成本及新增折舊仍對毛利率
形成壓力，加上海外晶圓廠持續稀釋毛利率，但先進製程需求強勁有助維持高
產能利用率，且 N3 製程量產規模擴大，小幅上調毛利率預估 0.1 個百分點至
66.3%(-0.1ppts QoQ)。調高營業利益至 9,911.86 億元(+13.1% QoQ)，上修
稅後淨利至 8,315.29 億元(+8.9% QoQ)，EPS 32.07 元，營運續創歷史新高。

### Page 2

報告內容 
台積電 3Q26 受惠 AI 及先進製程需求強勁，優於預期且創歷史新高 
受惠 AI 相關需求持續強勁 ，Agentic AI 逐步由傳統聊天機器人朝 AI 助理發展 ， 帶動AI 加速器、
CPU 及網通晶片需求同步增加，加上智慧型手機新產品備貨需求，以及 N2 製程自 4Q25 量產
後持續擴大產能，帶動 3Q26 HPC 與智慧型手機營收分別季增 22%及 10%，占整體營收比重
達 68%及 21%。以製程別觀察，N2 營收占晶圓營收比重由 2Q26 的 3%提升至 8%，N3 亦由
30%提升至 33%，N7 及以下先進製程合計占比達 79%，顯示先進製程需求持續成為主要成長
動能，3Q26 以 12 吋約當晶圓計算之出貨量季增 3.2%至 447.6 萬片。 
在先進製程占比提升及晶圓出貨量增加帶動下 ， 營收1.494 兆元(+17.6% QoQ)， 優於預期3.0%。
雖然 N3 產能利用率維持高檔，加上整體營運規模持續擴大，惟 N2 初期量產仍面臨較高的製
造成本與折舊負擔 ， 形成稀釋 ，但稀釋程度低於我們原先預期 ，毛利率 66.4%(-1.3ppts QoQ)。
隨 N2、A14 等先進製程持續開發，研發費用增加至 889.79 億元，推升營業費用至 1,159.34
億元(+17.1% QoQ)，營業利益 8,764.45 億元(+15.1% QoQ)，營業利益率 58.7%，稅後淨利
7,633.41 億元(+8.0% QoQ)，EPS 29.43 元，優於預期且創歷史新高。

預期台積電 4Q26 受惠 AI 需求持續擴張，N2 放量推升營運再創新高 
AI 相關需求延續強勁成長趨勢，隨 CSP 持續增加 AI 基礎建設投資，AI 加速器、Agentic AI 相
關CPU 及網通晶片對先進製程的需求持續增加 ， 加上N2製程於新竹及高雄廠區持續擴大產能，
預期將成為 4Q26 主要營收成長動能。雖然部分消費性電子及價格敏感型終端市場受到零組件
價格上漲與總體經濟不確定性影響，需求相對疲弱，但 HPC 與智慧型手機對 N2、N3 等先進
製程的需求仍維持強勁，且台積電持續透過 N5 設備轉換支援 N3，以及 N7、N5、N3 跨製程
節點產能優化，提高晶圓產出與產能利用效率。

### Page 3

上修 4Q26 營收預估 9.5%至 1.699 兆元(+13.8% QoQ) 。N2 處於快速擴產階段，初期量產成
本及新增折舊仍對毛利率形成壓力 ， 加上海外晶圓廠生產成本相對較高 ， 持續帶來毛利率稀釋，
但先進製程需求強勁有助維持高產能利用率，且 N3 製程量產規模擴大與生產效率改善有助降
低單位製造成本，小幅上調毛利率預估 0.1 個百分點至 66.3%(-0.1ppts QoQ) 。隨先進製程研
發投入增加、全球產能擴張及營運規模持續成長，調升營業費用預估 16.9%至 1,364.36 億元
(+17.7% QoQ)，調高營業利益至 9,911.86 億元(+13.1% QoQ)，營業利益率 58.3%，上修稅
後淨利預估 7.3%至 8,315.29 億元(+8.9% QoQ)，EPS 32.07 元，營運續創歷史新高。

先進製程與 CoWoS 持續供不應求，預期台積電 26 年營運創新高 
隨全球 CSP 持續擴大 AI 資料中心建設，AI 加速器、客製化 ASIC 及高速網通晶片需求持續增
加，加上 Agentic AI 逐步由聊天機器人朝具備自主執行能力的 AI 助理發展，進一步帶動 CPU
及先進製程運算晶片需求，推升台積電 HPC 業務持續成長。製程方面，N3 持續受惠 HPC、智
慧型手機及 AI 相關需求，產能利用率維持高檔，N2 自 4Q25 正式量產後，於 26 年持續擴大
新竹及高雄廠區產能，逐步提高先進製程營收貢獻。CoWoS 先進封裝需求持續強勁，台積電
已具備 5 倍光罩尺寸封裝量產能力，並朝 9 倍及 14 倍光罩尺寸推進，以滿足 AI 加速器晶片尺
寸與 HBM 整合需求持續提升的趨勢，預估 26 年年底 CoWoS 月產能達 14 萬片。 
在先進製程及先進封裝需求同步成長下 ，上修26 年營收預估3.5%至5.598 兆元(+47.0% YoY)。
受惠 N3 製程產能利用率提升、產品組合朝高階製程轉移，以及整體營運規模擴大帶來的製造
效率與成本改善，有助抵銷N2 初期量產及海外晶圓廠成本稀釋，其中海外廠預估對26 年全年
毛利率造成約 2 個百分點影響，維持資本支出 620 億美金之預估，上調毛利率預估 0.2 個百分
點至 66.7%(+6.8ppts YoY) 。調升營業利益預估 3.4%至 3.286 兆元(+69.8% YoY) ，調高稅後
淨利預估 3.8%至 2.874 兆元(+67.6% YoY)，EPS 110.83 元，營運創歷史新高。

### Page 4

預期台積電 27 年 N2 全面放量與全球產能陸續開出，營運再創新高 
隨全球 CSP 持續增加 AI 基礎建設投資 ，AI 運算需求由訓練逐步延伸至推論及 Agentic AI 應用，
帶動 AI 加速器、CPU 及網通晶片需求持續增加，使先進製程及先進封裝需求維持成長。製程
方面，我們預估 27 年 N2 全年產能較 26 年增加至 2.4 倍，主要由智慧型手機及 HPC/AI 客戶
需求推動，同時透過製造效率提升增加有效晶圓產出。N3 方面，受惠AI 及 HPC 客戶多年期需
求能見度提高，持續增加全球 N3 產能，南科新增 N3 晶圓廠預計 1H27 量產，且因客戶需求
持續增加，決定再新增一座 N3 廠，預計 1H28 量產；美國方面，亞利桑那第二廠已完成廠房
建設，目前進入設備搬入及安裝階段，預計 3Q27 開始量產，時程較原先規劃提前，日本 P2
則規劃於 28 年量產。隨 AI 加速器封裝尺寸持續增加，CoWoS 朝 9 倍、14 倍光罩尺寸發展，
CoPoS 亦持續開發，有望支援未來更大尺寸及更高整合度的 AI 晶片需求，27 年年底 CoWoS
月產能由原先預估之 19 萬片/月上修至 20 萬片/月。 
因先進製程持續供不應求，上修 27 年營收預估 12.3%至 8.143 兆元(+45.5% YoY)。為了因應
擴產需求，27 年資本支出由原先預估至 800 億美金上修至 850 億美金(+37.1% YoY) ，N2 持
續快速擴產、新增產能折舊增加，以及亞利桑那第二廠開始量產，海外晶圓廠成本稀釋壓力將
逐步提高，但上調 N3 及 N2 產能利用率，且先進製程占比提升有助維持產品組合優勢，調高
毛利率預估 0.5 個百分點至 66.5%(-0.1ppts YoY) ，調升營業利益預估 9.2%至 4.673 兆元
(+42.2% YoY)，營業利益率 57.4%，上修稅後淨利預估 7.8%至 3.918 兆元(+36.3% YoY)，EPS 
151.11 元，營運續創新高。

### Page 5

Agentic AI 擴大運算需求，台積電技術持續領先業界 
隨全球 CSP 持續擴大 AI 資料中心資本支出，GPU、AI ASIC、Agentic AI 相關 CPU 及網通晶
片需求同步成長 ， 台積電N3 產能利用率維持高檔 ，N2 進入快速放量階段 ， 雖然N2 初期量產、
海外晶圓廠擴張及新增折舊將對毛利率形成壓力，但先進製程營收占比提升、規模經濟效益、
生產效率改善及跨節點產能最佳化，有助於部分抵銷成本稀釋影響。亞利桑那州總投資規模 達
2,650 億美元，並持續評估德州擴產機會，反映公司對 AI 長期結構性需求的高度信心，亦有助
鞏固其在全球先進製程及高階晶圓代工市場的競爭優勢。 
目前台積電股價交易在 2,440 元(16.1X 2 7(F) PER) ，位於近三年歷史本益比參考區間 12~26x
中間偏下位置，考量預期台積電在 26~27 年間將持續實現強勁的營運增長 ，且 AI 由 Generative 
AI 邁向 Agentic AI，造成 Token數量暴增，運算需求大幅提升，但因 2 奈米量產爬坡以及海外
廠折舊稀釋毛利率，27 年毛利率將小幅衰退，故本益比由中間偏上之 23 倍下調至 22 倍，因
調高 27 年獲利預估，故維持強力買進投資建議，目標價由 3,220 元(23 x 修正前 FY27E EPS)
調升至 3,320 元(22 x 修正後 FY27E EPS)。

### Page 6

公司簡介 
台灣積體電路製造股份有限公司(TSMC)是全球領先的專業半導體代工廠，成立於 1987 年，總
部位於台灣新竹科學園區。TSMC 的核心業務是提供先進的晶圓製造服務，專注於為各大半導
體設計公司提供生產支援，而不從事自有品牌的產品設計或銷售。這使得 TSMC 能夠專注於技
術創新和製程優化，成為全球最重要的半導體代工廠之一。

自成立以來，TSMC 便致力於開創並發展純晶圓代工模式，這一模式徹底改變了半導體產業的
競爭格局，並且成為全球各大半導體設計公司合作的首選。公司在製程技術上的不斷創新，使
得其在全球市場中保持著領先地位 ， 尤其在先進製程領域如3 奈米和即將量產的 2 奈米製程上，
TSMC 更是無可匹敵的領頭羊。

圖 2. Revenue by Application

資料來源: TSMC、國泰證期研究部整理

TSMC 的客戶遍及全球，涵蓋了許多領先的半導體設計公司，包括蘋果、英偉達、超微、聯發
科等。這些企業依賴 TSMC 的製程技術來生產高效能運算、智能手機、AI 加速器以及車用電子
等各類產品。公司長期以來不斷擴大其生產能力，並在台灣及全球設立了多座先進的製造廠，
這些廠房均採用最先進的技術，並且能夠應對不斷增加的市場需求。

截至最近幾年，TSMC 的年產能已達 1600 至 1700 萬片 12 吋晶圓，並且在全球半導體市場的
市佔率持續穩居領先地位。這一市場地位使得 TSMC 不僅是全球晶圓代工市場的領導者，同時
也是半導體生態系統中不可或缺的核心企業。

### Page 7

台積電 ESG 政策 
台積電主要從事晶圓代工，產業類別歸類在半導體行業中，依據專門為投資人設計的永續揭露
框架 SASB 準則，半導體行業關注重點為下列幾項：溫室氣體排放、能源管理、水和廢水管理、
廢棄物和危險材料管理、員工健康與安全、員工多元化和包容性、產品設計與生命週期管理、
材料採購與效率。由於台積電有設立工廠從事生產，研究部將篇幅著重在溫室氣體排放、水和
廢水管理 ， 並因員工對於晶圓代工而言是相當重要的資產 ， 故員工相關議題也會著重於下描述。

1. 溫室氣體排放： 
民國 111 年台積公司溫室氣體總排放量約 1,800 萬公噸二氧化碳當量，來自含氟溫室氣體與
氧化亞氮等範疇一製程直接排放占 11%；電力使用造成的範疇二能源間接排放占 50%，仍是
最大排放源 ； 以原物料生產 、 能源上游相關活動與運輸為主的範疇三價值鏈間接排放則占 39%。
因先進製程產 
能持續擴充，溫室氣體整體排放較前一年上升 14%，但考量產能增長比例，單位產品排放量則
較前一年微幅下降 ， 顯示台積公司持續精進低碳製造作為 ， 仍有效降低單位產品溫室氣體排放。

圖 3. 台積電溫室氣體排放淨零路程 
資料來源:TSMC、國泰證期研究部整理

2. 水和廢水管理： 
民國 111 年整體回收系統增加 335 萬立方公尺節水量，晶圓單位產品用水量 137.3(公升／十
二吋晶圓當量─光罩數)，相較於基準年(民國 99 年)的 140.9(公升／十二吋晶圓當量─光罩數)
下降 2.6%，未達年度目標，主因為民國 111 年成立新廠區且為試產階段，雖然產能尚不具經
濟規模，但仍需一定程度的用水量，導致節水率下降；至於單位產品廢水排放量則為  93.0(公
升／十二吋晶圓當量─光罩數)， 相較前一年上升 18%， 同樣與新建廠區試產階段用水需求有關 ，
在新建廠區產能達到經濟規模前，台積公司將持續優化回收水系統設備操作參數、提升用水效
率，降低廢水排放量。

### Page 8

圖 4. 歷年節水量                            圖 5. 水資源回收量以及回收率

資料來源:TSMC、國泰證期研究部整理             資料來源:TSMC、國泰證期研究部整理 
3. 員工健康與安全： 
民國 111 年台積公司安全績效指標藍燈(優等)比例佔86%， 較民國110 年比例再提升 9.8%，
主要改善在於被動指標的虛驚事件數自前一年的 28 件下降至 17 件，發生千人率由民國 110 
年的 0.252 下降至 0.145。當發生虛驚事件時， 除使用 3L5W 品質分析工具進行調查，亦透
過虛驚事件通報及調查程序，訪談事件相關人員、分析及檢查設備、物料測試、相關文件與記
錄查核或重建現場以鑑別事件的直接原因、間接原因與基本原因，主動挖掘避免類似事件再發
的改善機會。

圖 6. 歷年事件數

資料來源:TSMC、國泰證期研究部整理

4. 員工多元化和包容性： 
為促進人才多元化、廣納特殊專業人才，台積公司持續在全球招募海外菁英，多年來與美國麻
省理工學院、美國史丹佛大學、美國加州大學柏克萊分校、日本東京大學及多所國際頂尖大學
密切合作，透過共同規畫研究專案培養尖端研究生、提早接觸優秀學子，以利未來延攬加入台
積公司。此外，半導體人才招募範圍亦拓展至美國各城市、新加坡與馬來西亞，並首次擴及歐
洲資訊人才，藉由線上說明會與實體招募活動，吸引人才加入。 
民國 111 年，全球招募 1 萬 2,442 位新進員工，新增 7,817 個優質工作機會，提供員工有競爭
力的整體薪酬、優於法令的福利制度、安全健康的工作環境，讓同仁樂於投入工作、學習與成
長，獲得歸屬感及成就感；並透過新人關懷措施，適切回應其需求，成功降低新進員工離職率。

穩健基本面支持資金流入新興債
分析師／謝曉薇
研究範疇／新興主權債／新興企業債
新興市場債市投資攻略
出 刊 日 : 2026/9/29
熱門議題 / 投資關鍵
《聯準會升息並未牽動新興國家央行政策轉緊》
聯準會如市場預期在 9月FOMC會議上升息一碼至
3.75%~4%，美國10年期公債維持在 5%上下高位
震盪，不過資金並未逃離新興債市 ，截至9/16，資
金連續7週淨流入新興債市，其中以新興當地債淨流
入量為最。在資金支持下，即使7月以來美債殖利率
自4.37%一路攀升，新興當地債同期間僅下跌 0.4%，
新興主要貨幣主權債雖然跌幅達到2%，但利差仍維
持收斂走勢，也相對美債及成熟投資級債抗跌。
市場投資展望
資產 4Q26殖利率目標價 投資展望

新興主要貨幣主權債指數 6.70% 較高的殖利率及穩健基本面將使其在利率波動時更為穩健
新興主要貨幣企業債指數 6.64% A~BB級企業相對穩健

重點摘要 除了美國以外 ，主要成熟國家央行都採取了升息的
舉措：歐洲與日本央行也都在今年進行升息 ，英國
央行9月雖按兵不動，但態度也偏向鷹派，部分原因
來自全球通膨預期變化 ，這也和中東地緣紛擾不斷
有極大關係。全球新興國家除了中國和土耳其外，7
月通膨多明顯放緩 ，主要是能源價格下跌抵銷了大
部分3~5月通膨飆升的影響；然而趨動通膨放緩的
因素正在減弱 ，AI相關需求推升產品及大宗商品價
格，食品成本也有走升的現象 ，抵消服務業通膨的
降溫，加上近期中東局勢的升溫推動布蘭特原油來
到100美元以上 ，延續時間若拉長 ，能源價格恐將
反轉成為通膨上行壓力來源 ，尤其新興國家消費者
物價指數組成當中 ，能源相關占比相對成熟國家高 ，

長時間高油價將使部分新興市場通膨高於目標水平 ，
或限制央行政策放鬆的空間。
在美國利率上升的背景下 ，新興國家央行即便在國
內經濟有所放緩下 ，推動寬鬆貨幣政策的態度也將
更為謹慎，但這不代表新興國家將因此轉向升息 ，
例如與聯準會同一天召開利率會議的巴西央行便如
市場預期降息一碼至13.75%，主要是國內經濟放緩，
以及通膨數據和潛在通膨指標已跌破央行區間上限 ，
雖只是個案 ，但也顯示新興國家貨幣政策更趨向於
反應自身經濟環境 。在中東地緣政治仍可控的情境
下，除了巴西以外 ，土耳其、匈牙利、以色列等新
興國家目前預期仍朝降息方向發展 ，惟降息空間可
能收斂，其他新興國家則預估維持目前的利率政策
不變，雖然有部分亞洲國家在 AI需求帶動下擁有強
勁的經濟成長動能而給予升息的條件 ，但AI暢旺的
需求也使其經濟體更具對抗利率上升的韌性。

不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行並不負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資之風險，並就投
資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
重點摘要 《利率已反應升息 ，基本面良好下高利吸引力更佳 》
從基本面角度來看 ，更趨強勁的經濟成長 、不斷上
升的通膨風險 、以及新興國家央行轉鷹的擔憂均可
能減低新興債市的投資利基 。然而市場價格目前反
映的是央行持續升息的預期 ，除了美國反應未來
2~3碼的升息空間外 ，利率期貨同樣顯示未來 12個
月波蘭、墨西哥及韓國央行會有3碼以上的升息空間，
但其實主要券商對貨幣政策調整的看法仍審慎 ，即
使是升息可能性較高的韓國 ，美國銀行及摩根大通
也僅預估升息幅度約2.5碼，市場過度反應將使目前
接近歷史高點的新興當地債殖利率更具下行空間；
此外，美國長天期公債拋售賣壓已基本釋放 ，加上
美元指數升幅有限的預估下 ，新興國家匯率走勢穩

健，拉美匯率更大幅貢獻投資當地債的美元報酬率 ，
這都是本行仍對新當債維持正向看法的原因。
雖然今年新興主要貨幣主權債指數總報酬表現平淡 ，
但反映的是下半年美國無風險利率的上升 ，利差其
實處於2007年以來偏低水準，顯現主權債對抗外部
不確定的韌性 。雖然第四季金融市場仍有不少風險 ，
包括成熟國家利率的波動 、高燃油價格及美國期中
選舉/巴西大選等政策風險，均可能使新興債市短線
承壓，然目前新興主要貨幣主權債殖利率接近7%，
以歷史經驗來看 ，在高利水準的支撐下 ，即使在利
差及殖利率同步擴大下 ，新興主權債月報酬跌勢也
會較4％~5%時要來得低 。中東地緣政治對油價的
干擾則是後續需持續關注的因子之一 ，但在基本面
持續向好及通膨可望於明年放緩的基準情境下 ，若
美國僅是短暫的溫和式升息 ，外在因素干擾應不會
影響投資人對新興債市的長期投資偏好。

重點摘要 《新興企業違約率預估可望維持在低位》
今年以來共有10家新興市場企業違約 ，平均分散在
三大主要新興市場 ，不過以債券規模來看 ，因為巴
西化工廠Braskem及綜合能源商Raizen均有50億美
元以上的債券違約規模 ，因此拉美區以票面金額計
算的違約率今年已來到6.2%為三大區域中最高，推
升新興市場非投資級企業 2026年違約率來到 2.7%。
不過今年以來的違約事件主要由債務重組 、豁免契
約、延期、暫停協議和不良資產置換等方式主導 ，
多數已完成重組 ，其他還可能發生的違約事件市場
價格也大致反應，摩根大通將2026年全年違約率預
估值自３月 4.3%下調至3.1%、持平於2025年全年；
其中新亞違約率預估降至 1.0%、為三大新興市場中

最低，就連2025年以前違約率動輒逾兩成的中國地
產債，今年亦沒有任何違約事件的發生。
中國房市價格雖止穩但未見到復甦跡象 ，新屋銷售
依然低迷，僅二手房需求出現改善 。中國官方近期
推行「先建後售 」制，以從根本上防範交付風險 ，
但若現行預售制取消 ，可能導致簽約銷售量下降 ，
雖有助於從供給面穩定房價 ，但對擁有大規模儲地
的地產開發商恐不利 ，一些流動性不佳的地產商可
能因商業模式的轉變及資金缺口造成資產週轉率下
降及槓桿率上升 。不過中國民營地產商在多年大規
模違約後多已被剔除在債券指數之外 ，指數僅存資
產負債表較強的國有企業為多 ，而這些國企和資本
較雄厚的開發商可望經由整合獲得市占 ，也較容易
透過REIT等管道融資 ，因此中國地產債或可能出現
重覆違約 ，但對亞洲債市影響不大 ，摩根大通預估
新興企業違約風險至2027年均將維持在低位。

估將來到3.1%

重點摘要 《亞洲企業較有能力對抗高利率》
另一方面，在目前全球市場對高利率環境感到擔憂
的同時，亞洲企業算是較有能力因應的族群 。從外
部融資角度來看 ，亞洲國家擁有強勁的經常帳盈餘
及外匯存底 ，往往較能抵禦外部風險 ，再者亞洲企
業資產負債表普遍相對較佳 ，發行的美元債存續期
間也相對其他區域短 ，美國公債利率上升對持有亞
債的投資人而言傷害相對較小；就國內融資而言 ，
雖然亞洲國家當地利率也在上升 ，但仍多相對美國
要來的低 ，融資成本較便宜 ，且亞洲債市當地投資
人需求高、流動性佳,在海外高利的環境下 ，多了一
條融資的管道。
觀察近年新興企業債指數利差與美國公債殖利率的
變動走勢多呈反向 ，即新興企業債利差能在一定程
度上抵消利率上升的影響 ，使得總回報仍可為正 ，

或降低負報酬衝擊。雖然今年3月時受到中東衝突爆
發和油價飆升 ，使得企業債利差與殖利率同步擴大 ，
這也是過往較常見到利率與利差同向的情況 ，但金
融市場對中東地緣政治風險的反應已鈍化 ，除非戰
事再擴大造成運力長時間中斷 ，否則油價大幅再上
漲空間也有限 。只不過目前信用債利差已來到非常
低的位置，離2006年底的歷史低位僅剩10個基點左
右的空間，這確實限縮利差再收斂空間 ，然而這反
應的是美國政府信評已從當時的的 AAA降至AA+，
市場還在擔心 政府債務持續成長 ，對企業信用基本
面反而更具信心 ，使得兩者間利差進一步縮小 ，雖
然下行空間有限 ，但企業債在低存續期間 、損益兩
平點更高 、基本面更佳及外部脆弱性更低的支持下 ，
對抗風險的能力預期也將更佳。

公債殖利率的變動走勢多呈
反向 ，一定程度抵消利率上
升影響
